# App control bindings

Each entry identifies the observed or explicitly supplemental control, its interaction record and any target-specific clarification. Labels grouped in one paragraph share that precise record. Shared chrome is listed where it appears; it never creates a second artifact owner. Source ordinal numbers help locate the inspected static occurrence, and are not production IDs.

<a id="h.kmlc6vrj1uj6"></a>

[IX-COLLAB-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.w5q04lw5pz1p) through 006 govern scope, commit and work lifecycle.

Version history → [IX-EDITORS-005](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.s4biwivw4a4w). Mounted control #10 (button): owner scope is selected canonical file or organisation metadata. Without a declared history adapter, show History unavailable; never invent a surrogate document or replay completed effects.

Comments → [IX-EDITORS-006](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1djno_7ptJQNPuuQyqUWOMxrmdhflZ3GKgHevnCJJBLc/edit?tab=t.yh4mk9q4cw20#heading=h.jtipjppc4jw3). Mounted control #11 (button): use an owner-provided stable anchor on selected canonical file or organisation metadata. Without a comment adapter, show Comments unavailable; this command does not send mail/chat, modify an action receipt or create an implicit document.

Contract-defined semantic field/region, not counted as an observed HTML control. The named record supplies its required behaviour; a distinct accessible Atlas control or equivalent semantic region must be registered and verified. Styled source appearance alone does not prove a working handler.

Author in Binder; Reference a file; Search authorised files; Candidate original → [IX-LINK-004](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tBC60DTC70QAZha8o7wn1SvgK1V3qL6vvKxDapYExl0/edit?tab=t.kh6hghkncf6x#heading=h.6xr53wu3edmh). Contract-defined semantic field/region, not counted as an observed HTML control. The named record supplies its required behaviour; a distinct accessible Atlas control or equivalent semantic region must be registered and verified. Styled source appearance alone does not prove a working handler.
